- **vnc-service**: Persistent virtual display + VNC for headless servers. Requires Debian/Ubuntu, systemd, sudo. VNC access restricted to local network via ufw firewall. Use whenever browser interaction is needed on a headless server — OAuth, CAPTCHA, 2FA. Other skills should call /vnc-service:setup as a prerequisite check and /vnc-service:run before browser operations. Use this skill when a tool needs browser interaction on a headless server — OAuth login, CAPTCHA solving, 2FA prompts. Trigger on: "vnc setup", "set up vnc", "headless browser", "no display", "DISPLAY not set", "CAPTCHA requires browser", "OAuth needs browser", or when any other skill calls /vnc-service:setup as a prerequisite. ([3D-Stories/vnc-service](https://github.com/3D-Stories/vnc-service/tree/HEAD/skills/status))
- **vnc-service:stop**: Stop the virtual display and VNC server services. Use when you no longer need browser interaction and want to free resources. - **Vocabulary Projection**: Vocabulary Projection methods interpret internal model states by projecting them through the unembedding matrix to obtain a distribution over the vocabulary. The core idea is that the unembedding matrix, which maps the final hidden state to output logits, can serve as a universal decoder for intermediate states throughout the model. By inspecting the top-ranked tokens of the resulting distribution, researchers can directly read off the semantic content encoded in any internal object — such as a residual stream state or an attention head output — in terms of the model's output vocabulary. ([zjunlp/Mechanist](https://github.com/zjunlp/Mechanist/tree/HEAD/skills/mechanism-skills/vocabulary-projection) · ★75)
